FILE: .\script.js
=========================================
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  
  // ---------- 1. Р“Р»Р°РІРЅР°СЏ ----------
  const progressFill = document.getElementById('progress-fill');
  const progressCount = document.getElementById('progress-count');

  if (progressFill && progressCount) {
    const progress = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('efwd-progress')) || {};
    const done = Object.values(progress).filter(Boolean).length;
    
    // Р’РђР–РќРћ: РўРµРїРµСЂСЊ Сѓ РЅР°СЃ 7 IT + 7 Life = 14 СѓСЂРѕРєРѕРІ
    const TOTAL_LESSONS = 14; 
    const percent = Math.min(100, Math.round((done / TOTAL_LESSONS) * 100));

    progressFill.style.width = percent + '%';
    progressCount.textContent = done;

    document.querySelectorAll('.lesson-card').forEach(card => {
      const href = card.getAttribute('href');
      if (!href) return;
      const match = href.match(/lesson-(\d+)\.html/);
      if (match && progress[`lesson-${match[1]}`]) {
        card.classList.add('is-done');
        const num = card.querySelector('.lesson-card__num');
        if (num) num.textContent = 'вњ“';
      }
    });
  }

  // ---------- 2. РЈСЂРѕРє ----------
  const lessonPage = document.querySelector('[data-lesson-id]');
  if (lessonPage) {
    const lessonId = lessonPage.dataset.lessonId;
    const btn = document.getElementById('complete-btn');

    if (btn) {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const p = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('efwd-progress')) || {};
        p[lessonId] = true;
        localStorage.setItem('efwd-progress', JSON.stringify(p));
        
        btn.textContent = 'вњ… РЈСЂРѕРє РїСЂРѕР№РґРµРЅ';
        btn.classList.add('is-done');
      });
    }
  }

  // ---------- 3. РљРЅРѕРїРєР° "РќР°РІРµСЂС…" ----------
  const scrollTopBtn = document.getElementById('scroll-top');
  if (scrollTopBtn) {
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
      if (window.scrollY > 300) scrollTopBtn.classList.add('show');
      else scrollTopBtn.classList.remove('show');
    }, { passive: true });

    scrollTopBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    });
  }

  // ---------- 4. РњРµРЅСЋ Рё РљРЅРѕРїРєРё ----------
  const menuToggle = document.getElementById('menu-toggle');
  const menuToggleInside = document.getElementById('menu-toggle-inside');
  const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
  const overlay = document.getElementById('overlay');

  function updateMenuIcon() {
    if (!menuToggle || !sidebar) return;
    menuToggle.innerHTML = sidebar.classList.contains('open') ? 'Г—' : 'в°';
  }

  if (sidebar) {
    if (window.innerWidth >= 1024) {
      sidebar.classList.add('open');
    }
    
    updateMenuIcon();

    function closeDesktopMenu() {
      document.body.classList.add('sidebar-closed');
      sidebar.classList.remove('open');
      updateMenuIcon();
    }

    function toggleSidebar() {
      const isDesktop = window.innerWidth >= 1024;
      let isOpen = false;
      
      if (isDesktop) {
        const isClosed = document.body.classList.contains('sidebar-closed');
        if (isClosed) {
          document.body.classList.remove('sidebar-closed');
          sidebar.classList.add('open');
          isOpen = true;
        } else {
          closeDesktopMenu();
          isOpen = false;
        }
      } else {
        isOpen = sidebar.classList.contains('open');
        if (isOpen) {
          sidebar.classList.remove('open');
          overlay.classList.remove('open');
          isOpen = false;
        } else {
          sidebar.classList.add('open');
          overlay.classList.add('open');
          isOpen = true;
        }
      }
      updateMenuIcon();
    }

    if (menuToggle) menuToggle.addEventListener('click', toggleSidebar);
    if (overlay) overlay.addEventListener('click', toggleSidebar);

    if (menuToggleInside) {
      menuToggleInside.addEventListener('click', closeDesktopMenu);
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar__nav a').forEach(link => {
      link.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (window.innerWidth < 1024 && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) {
          toggleSidebar();
        }
      });
    });
  }

  // ---------- 5. ScrollSpy ----------
  const sections = document.querySelectorAll('section[id]');
  const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar__nav a');

  if (sections.length > 0 && navLinks.length > 0) {
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
      let current = '';
      sections.forEach(section => {
        if (window.scrollY >= section.offsetTop - 120) {
          current = section.getAttribute('id');
        }
      });
      navLinks.forEach(link => {
        link.classList.remove('active');
        if (link.getAttribute('href') === `#${current}`) {
          link.classList.add('active');
        }
      });
    }, { passive: true });
  }

  // ---------- 6. РђР’РўРћРњРђРўРР§Р•РЎРљРђРЇ РџРћР”РЎР’Р•РўРљРђ РўР•РљРЈР©Р•Р“Рћ РЈР РћРљРђ ----------
  const topNavLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.lesson-top-nav a');
  const currentPath = window.location.pathname;

  if (topNavLinks.length > 0) {
    topNavLinks.forEach(link => {
      const href = link.getAttribute('href');
      if (href && href !== '#' && currentPath.includes(href)) {
        link.classList.add('active');
      }
    });
  }

});

const CACHE_NAME = 'english-lesson-cache-v8'; // Р’РµСЂСЃРёСЏ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅР° РґР»СЏ СЃР±СЂРѕСЃР° СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ РєРµС€Р°

// РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє Р’РЎР•РҐ С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РґР»СЏ РѕС„Р»Р°Р№РЅ-СЂРµР¶РёРјР°
const ASSETS = [
  './',
  './index.html',
  './style.css',
  './script.js',
  './manifest.json',
  // --- IT (7 СѓСЂРѕРєРѕРІ) ---
  './lessons/it/it-lesson-1.html',
  './lessons/it/it-lesson-2.html',
  './lessons/it/it-lesson-3.html',
  './lessons/it/it-lesson-4.html',
  './lessons/it/it-lesson-5.html',
  './lessons/it/it-lesson-6.html',
  './lessons/it/it-lesson-7.html',
  // --- LIFE (7 СѓСЂРѕРєРѕРІ) ---
  './lessons/life/life-lesson-1.html',
  './lessons/life/life-lesson-2.html',
  './lessons/life/life-lesson-3.html',
  './lessons/life/life-lesson-4.html',
  './lessons/life/life-lesson-5.html',
  './lessons/life/life-lesson-6.html',
  './lessons/life/life-lesson-7.html',
  // --- РРєРѕРЅРєРё ---
  './images/icons/icon-192.ico',
  './images/icons/og-preview.png'
];

// 1. РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР°: РєСЌС€РёСЂСѓРµРј РѕСЃРЅРѕРІРЅС‹Рµ С„Р°Р№Р»С‹
self.addEventListener('install', (e) => {
  e.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
      return cache.addAll(ASSETS);
    })
  );
  self.skipWaiting();
});

// 2. РђРєС‚РёРІР°С†РёСЏ: С‡РёСЃС‚РёРј СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Р№ РєСЌС€
self.addEventListener('activate', (e) => {
  e.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then((keys) => {
      return Promise.all(
        keys.map((key) => {
          if (key !== CACHE_NAME) {
            return caches.delete(key);
          }
        })
      );
    })
  );
  self.clients.claim();
});

// 3. РЎС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ Stale-While-Revalidate
self.addEventListener('fetch', (e) => {
  if (e.request.method !== 'GET') return;

  e.respondWith(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
      return cache.match(e.request).then((cachedResponse) => {
        const fetchPromise = fetch(e.request).then((networkResponse) => {
          cache.put(e.request, networkResponse.clone());
          return networkResponse;
        }).catch(() => null);

        return cachedResponse || fetchPromise;
      });
    })
  );
});
